class=\"pcpa-popup\">\n<h2>Late Filing for the 2026 Homestead Exemption</h2>\n<p>\n    If you missed the <strong>March 2, 2026</strong>, deadline to file for your Homestead Exemption, you may still have the opportunity to apply.<br />\n    While our online system is now closed for the 2026 tax year, you can visit our Palatka office to complete a <strong>&ldquo;Late File&rdquo;</strong><br />\n    application in person. Office Location: 312 Oak St Palatka, FL 32177\n  </p>\n<p><strong>To confirm your eligibility, please bring documents that verify the following:</strong></p>\n<h3>Eligibility Guidelines</h3>\n<ul>\n<li>You owned your home on or before <strong>January 1, 2026</strong>.</li>\n<li>You lived in and established the home as your primary residence on or before <strong>January 1, 2026</strong>.</li>\n<li>\n      You do not currently receive residency-based tax benefits (such as STAR, Enhanced STAR, Owner Credit, etc.) on any other property.\n    </li>\n</ul>\n<h3>Verification Documents</h3>\n<p>To assist with your application, please provide the following items showing your current home address:</p>\n<ul>\n<li>Florida Driver&rsquo;s License or Florida ID.</li>\n<li>Vehicle Registration.</li>\n<li>Voter Registration Card.</li>\n<li>Permanent Resident Card (for non-U.S. Citizens).</li>\n</ul>\n<p>\n    Should you have any questions about these items or your specific situation, please contact our office at<br />\n   386-329-0286.\n  </p>\n<p><a href=\"https://pamap.putnam-fl.gov/homestead/\"\n   style=\"color: #000; background-color: #FFFF00; padding: 2px 10px 4px onmouseout=\"this.style.backgroundColor='#FFFF00';\"><br />\n   Click Here E-File for 2027<br />\n</a>\n</div>\n\t\t</div>\n\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t<button type=\"button\" class=\"pum-close
